### L12 letters and a camera settle

Built on **92369320af2dd46e947e27a8210c5959fd3766a8**, with a clean incoming tree. Before writing, read AGENTS, CORRECTIONS-19, CORRECTIONS-17 G/R2/R3 and round 6's entrance/hold/exit/check record in that order, then the project READMEs, retained round 18/19 evidence and MOCK-A.diff. The mock is the approved motion reference, not a patch: no MOCK_SPEED, Replay or Slow motion controls were carried over. Pre-edit acceptance, original sources/hashes and the unchanged preview are in `.scratch/review-out/r19/l12-acceptance.md`, `l12-baseline.json` and `l12-before-preview.html`. L3 remains withdrawn; L7 continues to replace L6's curves and speed rule.

#### Timetable as built

All times below are seconds from the loader's actual last landing, unchanged from L5. Rises use **quint-out** `1-(1-p)^5`, letter weights **cubic-out** `1-(1-p)^3`, screen/camera **quart-out** `1-(1-p)^4`. There is no overshoot or new opacity fade.

| Part | Span and curve |
| --- | --- |
| Header D / G | **0–.70 / .08–.78**, quint-out; both G paths share its clock. Each starts **830 SVG units** below its fixed mark-box clip. |
| Journey / Contact | **.25–.85 / .33–.93**, quint-out; each top starts **6 px below its bar bottom**. Fixed bar clip, 40 px horizontal slack. |
| Danial | Letter j: **.30 + .055j to 1.15 + .055j**, j=0…5; last lands **1.425**. Quint-out rise, independent cubic-out **300→800** weight. |
| Endrawes | Letter j: **.50 + .055j to 1.35 + .055j**, j=0…7; last lands **1.735**. Same curves. |
| Lede | **23 words** in flow reading order. Word i starts **1.15 + .028i + (.12 if in the second part)**, lasts **.70**, quint-out. Greeting: starts 1.150/1.178/1.206; second part starts **1.354**, final word **1.886–2.586**. |
| Floor | **1.90–2.90**, unchanged EASE.wipe and hard whole-dot/pool circle. No star ring. |
| Screen | **2.20–3.50**, quart-out, from **4.5 world units below** its authored first frame. Loop zero is **3.50**. |
| Camera | **1.90–3.90**, one quart-out clock: extra **12° yaw / 5° pitch / .88 home scale** to the exact home view. |
| Five craft hairlines | **3.10–3.50, 3.20–3.60, 3.30–3.70, 3.40–3.80, 3.50–3.90**, EASE.wipe. |
| Four craft buttons | **3.172–3.772, 3.272–3.872, 3.372–3.972, 3.472–4.072**, quint-out; .60 s from 18% into each row's .40 s hairline. Each starts one measured row height down. |
| Scroll / Pause | **3.95–4.50 / 4.03–4.58**, quint-out, top starts 6 px below the footer bottom. Fixed footer clip with 40 px horizontal slack. |
| End | **4.60**; inline entrance effects/overlays/wrappers cleared, name reaction enabled, loop clock **1.10**. |

The exact two-group overlaps are **.30–.93** mark/nav + name; **1.15–1.735** name + lede; **1.90–2.586** lede + world; **3.10–3.90** world + crafts; **3.95–4.072** crafts + foot. Outside them at most one group moves. Floor/screen/camera are one world group; the loop after 3.50 is not an entrance group. **362** entrance samples (every 1/60 s plus exact boundaries) and **567** samples including the preceding explosion both find a maximum of **2**, with no exceptions.

#### Measurement, masks and handovers

The flow name stays two original text nodes at weight 800. A character Range measures each final pen box with its kerning intact. An aria-hidden absolute overlay draws generated-content letters at those positions, with the measured box height as its own line height. Generated content contributes no text nodes or flow boxes to `page-layout`'s ink walk. Each line has one fixed mask ending **3 px below its measured ink bottom**; its letters start **ink height +16 px** down. Only the overlay letters change weight. At that line's last landing the overlay is removed and the flow paint restored in the **same apply call**. Landed letters inherit 800; no weight is left on a flow line. The existing name reaction stays off through entrance/holds/reduced motion and starts after 4.60 as before.

The lede's unchanged text is walked with word Ranges, grouped by measured line/top and sorted left to right. Each generated-content word uses its exact left/top/width/height and **its own Range box height as line height**, preserving its baseline. Line masks run **2 px above to 2 px below** their measured boxes; words start **line height +6 px** down. At **2.586**, all lede masks/words are removed and flow paint restored together. Flow text remains the accessible text throughout.

All geometry and overlay construction happens at `measure` (boot, resize/fonts, or a new review hold), never in apply. The landing reuses the overlays already prepared behind the opaque loader instead of repeating measurement in the handover frame. Per-frame entrance paint writes transforms, clip paths and moving-letter weights; structural/class changes are limited to the prescribed handovers/cleanup. Resize and backwards review holds first restore flow and rebuild once. No DOM layout read occurs in the modeled 277 apply frames. Every transformed path/link/button and clipped container is cleared when the entrance ends or is cancelled. Holding exactly **4.60** also clears every entrance mask/overlay/wrapper in that same frame; the Node and external browser gates check that endpoint before releasing the hold.

**Craft detail beyond the scratch mock:** a neutral block wrapper gives each button its own fixed text mask exactly **2 px clear of both row hairlines**. The li's separate clip draws its hairlines. This prevents moving glyphs from leaking through a hairline strip, including the final row's bottom strip. The wrapper restores the original button directly to its li at cleanup; its accessible text and normal flow dimensions are retained. This implements L12's 2 px rule rather than adding a new look.

`liftPieces()` moves every piece and explicit shadow position by `-4.5*(1-screenProgress)` and translates each piece/carry clip plane by the same amount. The shared **[0,1,0,0]** floor plane clips colour, outline and the light's depth/shadow caster passes. Before 2.20 all first-frame corners are below it; no caster is retained. At 3.50 the lift returns the **original array itself**, exactly the retained round-6 `frameAt(0,{first:true})` Photoshop first pass. The loop then starts without skipping time. Renderer/shader code remains byte-identical.

`settleView()` pivots about **SCREEN's centre [0.2,2.4,-1.8]**, keeping that point's home screen coordinates fixed while yaw, pitch and scale settle. At 3.90 it returns the **original home view object**, with exact S/cx/cy/camera vectors. `layout.cover` always receives the home view, so the stage target/fit never follows the turning camera. Stars retain screen-space coordinates. `__hero.entrance(t)` retains exact-time holds and its pieces getter now returns the same lifted pieces that were drawn; view/diagnostic projection follows the current camera. Reduced motion, `?t=`, click/seek cancellation and the loader/pointer contract are retained.

#### Checks and negative controls

`tools/l12-checks.mjs` adds **241** curve/clock probes, **2,766** world/plane/pivot checks (240 Hz, desktop/phone, default and changed home camera), and **277** real DOM-view apply samples on deterministic ranges. The range model deliberately contains kerning; it checks original flow text, pen boxes, generated content, exact mask/drop numbers, zero per-frame layout reads, allowed style writes, flow handovers, wrapper restoration and cleanup/rebuild. It also checks the actual player's home-coverage/lift/camera wiring and all three renderer reveal-pass uniform sites. The retained `entrance-checks` adds the complete L12 timetable and still runs all loader/field/handover/player/label checks.

New controls are tested in memory, never by editing source on disk:

No large style/layout, main paint, garbage collection or JS span fills either final desktop gap. The load gap overlaps native raster flushing during gathering; the entrance gap overlaps **RasterDecoderImpl::DoEndRasterCHROMIUM::Flush 63.86 ms**, with only **.29 ms** in tick. The trace does not expose that command's internal split into driver compilation/upload. It would be inaccurate to call it a JS or letter-weight stall, or to claim the new held-endpoint cleanup caused it (that branch is not executed in this normal-play recording). The loader, artwork and renderer remain unchanged under L12. No further change to them or to glyph raster quality was justified by this unlocalized native cost. No delay, altered clock or unrelated rewrite was introduced to move the gap out of scope. Thus the strict desktop entrance target remains **unfulfilled**; Node all-clean is a separate logic result.

## Round 19, L13

Built L13 parts 1-4 on the supplied L12 files. The final Node suite prints **all clean**; every retained control and every new L13 control is caught at its named gate. Final full-suite log: [all-clean-reverified.txt](.scratch/l13/all-clean-reverified.txt). No publication or site build was run.

### Reference and acceptance record

Read CORRECTIONS-19's L13, L11 and L12, MOCK-X.diff, README and BUILD's L11/L12 records before implementation. The mock was studied for its numbers and appearance, without applying it. Used the installed motion-playbook locally. Acceptance was: explosion-only tapers; one 0.04 s window with unchanged nominal widths; continuous loader taper coverage against Canvas; the exact exit timetable; a reversible shared red/asset step; complete per-object blocks; no sinking or below-floor geometry; projected front-pass sparks; retained loader, entrance, artwork, layout, colours, dynamics, labels and reduced-motion behavior. The explicit prohibition on reviewer agents takes precedence over the standing agent-review workflow.

### Runtime changes and numbers

- Field stars now have an exploding flag and a separate elapsed explosion clock. Hold release and an export kick set it. Capture and arrival home clear it. At least 0.05 s after a kick, the first taper no longer exceeding the largest actually drawn copy's width clears it permanently for that explosion. Stirring changes neither flag nor explosion clock. Its existing impulse, mass, drag, caps and front-copy return stay unchanged.
- History records home + own offset. Parallax and scrolling still position heads, with zero trail upload for a non-exploding star. The export kick measures stars at their displayed parallax and scroll positions.
- STREAK_WINDOW in loader-stars.mjs is the single constant: 1/50 = 0.02 s -> 2/50 = 0.04 s. Loader burst, field history and export sparks import/read it. Gathering flights now have no tail or streak. Individually landed loader stars have an exact zero trail. All loader launch, wind-up, explosion and handover clocks stay unchanged.
- Loader WebGL requests antialias: false -> true. Multisampling alone failed the pixel gate, so the final painter also computes the original triangle's edge coverage in the fragment shader. Its alpha transition spans 1.2 device pixels, centred on the original edge; the geometric width and head diameter stay unchanged. A two-triangle raster quad has a 2-device-pixel padding solely to invoke coverage. The shader receives the original head, tail and width. The dot-head shader stays unchanged for the field handover. Canvas fallback geometry is retained. There is no width multiplier or speed-driven growth rule.
- assetWipe is the sole red/asset transition function, used forward at arrival and backward at exit. It retains the world-x wipe, EASE.wipe, with opposite planes on asset and red. Every visible object's separated bounding box supplies its own red volume, including glass, hardware and each reading half. Arrival split endpoints use those same complete bounds. Boundary amounts within 1e-12 of 0/1 snap to their exact endpoint to avoid a floating-point red sliver in an exact held frame.
- The exit starts at the previous off[0]: wipe 0.45 s, formats 0.10 s apart, finished at 0.65; merge with EASE.wipe 0.65-1.15; squash to 0.78 radius 1.15-1.33; swell to 1.15 with EASE.exit 1.33-1.45; no ball/export geometry in the 1.45 frame; scene end 1.50. BALL.sink retains its table name but changes 1.0 -> 1.5 s. The old sink's translations, floor clips and sinkTop renderer cull are removed. Figma's fixed clockLength changes 18.545162584479227 -> 19.045162584479227. The loop is 99.49089474781469 -> 101.49089474781469 s.
- Sparks: 0 -> 140, with exactly 98 red and 42 paper. The mock's upper-hemisphere directions, low-biased distribution, 5-16 units/s, tau 0.13-0.24 s braking, 1.5-3 px heads and 0.7-1.25 s lives are retained; size goes to zero over the last 45%. They use a separate buffer batch in the existing star program's front pass, after the scene, with full brand colours. There is no sparks canvas. Pure loop-time sampling carries them into the next opening; forward playback kicks nearby field stars once per crossed burst, at reach 2.6 * stage scale and 0.75 * hold base impulse with squared falloff. The first entrance excludes a previous-cycle burst that has not happened. Reduced motion suppresses sparks and retains held stills.

**Count conflict resolved explicitly:** the brief says four InDesign red pieces and later three red blocks, but its per-object requirements identify five objects: the five-copy stack, left reading half, right reading half, phone and tablet. This build gives all five their own exact red volume, under three format clocks. The other scenes have three red volumes. All five InDesign volumes merge into the single ball. A four-piece implementation would have to omit a required half or combine unlike objects. The exit stack is 0.25 high (five copies), replacing the mock's 0.30 six-copy block. Reading halves are 0.04 and 0.01 high. This is the sole count decision; it is visible in the sheets and checked explicitly.

### Controlled-clock gates and negative controls

Playwright's installed local clock drives real Edge at 1440 x 900 with a 1440 x 900 hero and 1,200 field stars. Scrolling adds a sibling below main, preserving hero height. The final run has 1567 recorded submitted-field frames. It records zero eligible tapers during stirring, parallax, scroll and a three-second hold. That hold forms the mark (435 assigned, 79 arrived). Since its captures do not yet fill 600 points, an additional eight-second hold exercises all 600 arrived points and 600 outer-ring stars, also with zero tapers. Release has 2182 taper submissions within its first .05 s, 32412 across the recorded release, and zero later revivals after a taper first disappears.

The speed-only countermodel restores trail eligibility and displayed-position history in memory. It fails **L13 stir: no taper** (370) and **L13 hold: no taper** (29061); it also produces 152662 tapers during the full ring hold and 23000 return revivals. The loader's real main-thread fallback runs under the same clock: 199 gathering frames with zero tapers, 106 burst frames, maximum 595 tapers (305 total frames). Worker timing is measured separately in the real-time trace.

The shared-window memory test changes .04 -> .08 s and observes all three consumers change: loader total trail 22060.261286 -> 46878.598255, field displacement 40 -> 80, spark component total 1993.847208 -> 4457.305655. Each is nonzero; no caller has a copied window constant.

New pure controls: old tail -> L13 loop; late burst -> L13 burst; missing reading halves and undersized red block -> L13 wipe full per-object bounds; restored downward motion -> L13 per-object bounds. The final suite also catches all 29 entrance/L12 controls, the L11 dots-only control, four round8 controls, 40 round17 controls, and all other retained controls at their gates. Logs retain actual error text; no fault is written to product files.

### Loader pixel gate

Same Edge GPU, identical loaderDots states at burst elapsed .04, .10, .30, .70 and 1.20 s, WebGL and retained Canvas fallback. Sample every quarter device pixel along every taper centre line, bilinearly between pixel centres. For Canvas alpha > .1, require WebGL >= half that alpha. Alpha differences below are normalized 0-1, 99th percentile over qualifying centre-line samples.

| Size | DPR | Samples | Positive gaps | P99 absolute alpha difference | Raw antialias-false control gaps |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| 1440 x 900 | 1 | 268188 | 0 | 0.251391 | 17428 |
| 1440 x 900 | 2 | 542026 | 0 | 0.195297 | 17426 |
| 390 x 844 | 1 | 143644 | 0 | 0.263297 | 9100 |
| 390 x 844 | 2 | 288440 | 0 | 0.203703 | 9756 |

Multisampling alone left 5,574 / 5,615 desktop gaps and 2,359 / 2,666 phone gaps (DPR 1 / 2, nearest-pixel diagnostic); it was insufficient. Shader edge coverage was selected on the strict pixel evidence even though the intermediate multisample trace added no new over-20 interval. This is a stated extension of the brief's performance-triggered fallback wording. The final gate uses the actual line location rather than repeatedly counting the same nearest pixel.

Because coverage is now supplied by the shader, the negative control restores the original raw triangle shader/geometry with antialias:false in memory. It is the original dashed-tail failure, rather than a context-flag-only toggle that leaves the replacement coverage active. All four control cases fail the alpha gate. This control adaptation is deliberate and recorded, with no weakened positive threshold.

### Reference, acceptance and changes

take-2.css applies user-select:none, -webkit-user-select:none, transparent tap highlights and -webkit-touch-callout:none universally. Existing name rules remain valid. tools/page.mjs needs no root change: selection is already empty for all specified gestures.

stars.mjs creates a fresh freePoints Set per hold. The first batch keeps its original pairing. Later batches repeatedly take the globally nearest available star/point pair. Only candidates whose nearest point was consumed need another search. hold.next remains the count assigned; overflow enters the existing ring. L13 already passed check 3, so explosion/capture eligibility and trail/blast code were left unchanged.

tools/l13b-checks.mjs adds all four browser checks and their controls; tools/checks.mjs invokes them. Controls mutate memory, with no fixture edits.

No earlier numerical expectation, tolerance, check body or negative-control mutation changed. tools/checks.mjs adds L13b and updates its header to identify its browser calls. Loop 101.49089474781469 s and exposure 0.04 s remain unchanged.

New gates require empty selection instead of selectable text, transparent instead of native tap tint, preserved activation/focus/accessibility, zero held/dragged tapers, release tapers within 0.05 s, closest free pairing instead of hold.next ordering, and <=2% crossings in each prescribed run. The fast gate stays strict and fails. README's latest status changes from L13's passing checkpoint to this explicit incomplete L13b status.

The setup matches L13's local Playwright resolution, Edge executable, GPU flags and persistent contexts. Profiles live under .scratch/l13b; non-file/data/blob page requests are blocked. Both sizes retain 2 px solid focus-visible outlines and accessible intro/craft text. Disabled craft handlers fail at craft 0 click at both sizes. Check 3 already passed in L13 and still passes: formed at 3 s, zero hold/figure-eight tapers, 5,616 taper submissions within 0.05 s of release. The 260 x 140 px figure eight lasts 2.5 s in 16 ms steps. Speed-only eligibility fails the drag with 227,532 submissions (also 57,041 during the hold).

### Reference and acceptance

Read CORRECTIONS-19 L14, L13 part 4, L12's name row and point 8, MOCK-T.md, README.md, and BUILD.md's L13/L13b records. Used the installed motion-playbook. MOCK-T supplied the approved pop/spark numbers only; no review panel, style switch, POP_SPOTS, extra canvas or mock globals were copied.

Acceptance before editing: one shared assetStep; exact per-object L13 blocks; connected reading halves as one scaling unit; 1 -> 1.08 with EASE.exit before k=0.30, cut, 1.08 -> 1 with EASE.enter; each pivot at its own footprint middle on the floor; no red/object mixture, edges, floor crossing or intersecting unit footprints. Exactly 25 pure loop-time spark events, 35 every-fourth burst directions each, top-edge origins frozen at the cut, no field push. Name rises, masks, pen positions and handoff preserved, inherited flow weight from its first frame, only transform/clip writes. The 101.491 s loop and the ball/split/hold/lid/merge/squash/swell/burst clocks remain.

### Runtime changes

stage.mjs renames assetWipe to assetStep. assetUnits computes connected components from visible objects' footprint boxes with the 0.02 gap. Each object's own objectBlock still supplies its red volume; both reading halves share one pivot and scale. Position and all three half sizes move together, preserving screens, pictures, glass and rotations. A 1e-12 k guard resolves exact cut boundaries after global-to-local subtraction.

popBursts derives its events from scene clocks and independently samples each last pre-cut pose (1e-8 seconds before the mathematical cut). It runs once at player setup and never accumulates playback state. Seeking backwards or starting mid-loop gives the same frozen top rectangle; later Figma lid motion cannot drag it. export-sparks.mjs samples the 35 directions with speed x0.3 and life x0.6, preserving tau, head size, color and last-45-percent shrink. Both puff and ball use the imported 0.04 s STREAK_WINDOW and the existing star front pass. Only events without top rectangles can kick the field, so puffs never do. Ball directions, 140 heads, braking, color and four pushes retain L13's checks.

entrance.mjs removes the weight member and the letter fontWeight write. The flow's inherited weight paints every rising generated-content letter. The rise clocks, masks, word overlay, camera and cleanup are unchanged.

## Round 19, L13c

L13c-1 amends only the fill deadlines in tools/l13c-checks.mjs: still 1.25 s -> 1.45 s; fast 0.5 s -> 0.75 s, both measured after formation. This follows CORRECTIONS-19.md and MOCK-U.md's method note: the required stage-centre and fast-path-start runs differ from the mock probe. The original L13c implementation record below is historical; current verification is recorded in the L13c-1 subsection at the end.

Implemented L13c items 1-3 and its replacement check, including the required negative control. The full node tools/checks.mjs command exits 1 for the two strict fill deadlines: still 1.328 s (limit 1.25 s), fast 0.576 s (limit 0.5 s). It does not print all clean. Both runs have zero crossing and zero far-side assignments. Every retained and new negative control is caught at its intended gate.

### Reference, acceptance and runtime changes

Read CORRECTIONS-19.md L13c, MOCK-U.md, L13b item 6 and check 4, BUILD.md's L13b/L14 records and README.md before editing. Used the installed motion-playbook. MOCK-U supplied rules and numerical context only; its diff was not pasted. Acceptance was recorded before editing in .scratch/l13c/ACCEPTANCE.md. The incoming offline page and source snapshots are retained there.

stars.mjs keeps the first batch's radial pairing. For later stars the nearest-point search admits only distances strictly less than the star's distance to the mark centre; the globally closest eligible pair is consumed first. Every waiting ring member is a candidate whenever points remain free, including frames without fresh captures. Captures with no eligible point join the ring. Assigned ring members are removed and marked non-ring. hold.next increments by take.length. The circular-mean target code was extracted unchanged into respaceRing and shared by joins and departures; radii, angular velocities and the existing easing are retained.

First-batch code and mappings are identical before/after (300 still, 312 fast). Source comparisons confirm unchanged circular-mean targets, turnRing, capture/growth/anchor follow, the controller, release, bursts and trails. All other runtime modules, CSS, artwork and clocks retain their incoming hashes.

### Every earlier changed expectation

| Check | Old expectation -> new expectation |
| --- | --- |
| L13b check 4 flight ceiling | At most 2 percent farther than centre distance -> at most 1 percent in each prescribed run. The existing 1e-8 px numerical comparison tolerance stays. |
| L13b check 4 destinations/fill | No far-side or fill-time assertion -> angle strictly under 90 degrees for every later assignment, all 600 points assigned within 1.25 s still / 0.5 s fast after formation. Both strict fill gates remain active and fail. |
| Assignment observation/oracle | Newly captured ids only, nearest among their free pairs -> all newly assigned stars, including ring departures, observed before damping; exhaustive closest-eligible-pair oracle over captures and ring members. |
| L13b check 4 control | Farthest-point hold.next order, no nearest search -> L13b nearest-free-only rule, without ring candidates, must fail the fast flight gate. The superseded control and its gate are replaced together. |
| Round16 R2 order | Compare the new ring against every prior member -> compare the cyclic order of members that remain after departures. The sum-of-angle-gaps check and its 1e-6 tolerance stay. |
| Round16 R2 re-spacing | Detect a join only from a net count change; m = new count minus old count; inspect all prior targets -> detect actual joins and departures, including simultaneous equal-count replacement; m = joined plus departed; inspect remaining members' targets. The 1.5 * max(1, m/2) slot limit stays. |
| Round16 R2 settling/report | Settle 1.5 / 2.0 s after the last net-count join -> after the last membership change. All radius, target, jitter, gap and 1 percent spin limits stay; output labels identify membership changes. Legacy result field names joins/lastJoin remain for compatibility. |
| tools/checks.mjs | Invoke L13b checks 1-4 -> invoke unchanged L13b checks 1-3 plus the separate L13c check/control. |

No other earlier check expectation, numeric tolerance or control mutation changed. L13b checks 1-3 and every L14 check retain their check bodies and limits. L13b's unused assignment instrumentation and replaced check-4 helpers were removed; its streak observation remains.

### Before and after: both prescribed runs

Both are controlled-clock 1440 x 900, 9 s holds. Still uses the actual stage centre (1004.7890625, 458), exactly as L13b check 4 did. Fast begins at (1320, 450), with no pre-hold, then follows the specified formula once per 16 ms frame; the final 8 ms completes 9 s. Both use ?t=1 review bypass. Flight = c.e0 at assignment, centre distance = length of point plus initial offset, and far side = a nonpositive dot product. Percentiles use floor(p * (n - 1)); lengths are CSS px. Times are from pointer-down; fill delta is filled minus formed.

| still | After | 300 | 0 (0.00%) | 0 | 314.802 | 421.606 | 459.041 | 2.360 | 3.688 | 1.328 |
| fast | Before | 288 | 125 (43.40%) | 110 | 393.366 | 556.605 | 599.181 | 0.455 | 0.919 | 0.464 |
| fast | After | 288 | 0 (0.00%) | 0 | 348.123 | 520.079 | 574.627 | 0.455 | 1.031 | 0.576 |

The exhaustive oracle confirms all 588 later choices and finds no eligible pair left waiting. One still and 21 fast assignments come from the ring; four fast assignment frames have only ring candidates. Every star and point is assigned at most once. All 600 points are ultimately assigned in each run. The nearest-only/no-ring fast control reproduces 125/288 crossings (43.40 percent), 110 far-side endpoints and a 0.464 s fill delta, and fails specifically at L13c flights: fast.

### Why the fill deadlines remain incomplete

On the last sampled still frame before the 1.25 s deadline, held time is 3.616666667 s: only 596 stars have been captured, with 592 points assigned and four stars waiting. There are 600 points. Even the incoming unrestricted rule cannot fill before 1.296 s after formation. Changing assignment cannot provide the missing captured stars without changing the first batch or gather behavior that L13c explicitly preserves. The new rule finishes at 1.328 s.

At the fast deadline, held time 0.960666667 s, 574 points are assigned and 61 stars wait for the remaining 26 points. The independent oracle confirms that no eligible pair was skipped; ring turn and mark motion make the last pairs available at 0.576 s after formation. A separate pure simulation using the unchanged 16 ms controller math reproduces 1.328 / 0.576 s. A diagnostic still hold at MOCK-U's (900, 450) gives 1.248 s; it does not replace the required actual-stage-centre run.

The source rule is fully built. No capture acceleration, ring retiming, reassignment, changed first batch, pre-hold, shortened path, lowered point count, deferred measurement start, loosened threshold or skipped failing gate was used to force all clean. Meeting these two deadlines needs a change to a preserved behavior or an amended gate. That part of the requested completion condition is left unmet under the explicit scope rules.

L14's 888 geometry samples, 875 puff heads, name geometry/weight checks, three contact sheets, controls and six native frame windows all pass. All six frame windows have zero intervals over 20 ms.

The new check uses the same installed local Playwright resolution, Edge executable, GPU/default flags and offline preview setup as the other page checks. Persistent profiles live only under .scratch. Non-file/data/blob page requests are blocked; attempted page requests and page errors are zero. Every context closes in finally. Early probe attempts timed out because the prior page's controlled clock was not resumed before closing; the corrected harness resumes it and activates each page before the next run. No product timing was altered by that correction.

## Round 19, L15

Built items 1-4 and the L15 check/control on the supplied L13c-1/L14 files. The complete node tools/checks.mjs run exits 0 and prints all clean; all retained and new negative controls are caught.

### Reference and implementation

Read CORRECTIONS-19.md L15 and the Round 19 BUILD records for shared field generation, angular loader pairing, launch-speed durations, worker/fallback handover, L13c-1 and L14, plus README. The incoming page is saved in .scratch/l15/before.html. Reference-derived acceptance was saved before edits in .scratch/l15/ACCEPTANCE.md. Used the installed motion-playbook; the local UI routing skill was read, with its npx CLI omitted under the install/network restriction.

star-labels.mjs measures Range.getClientRects on the four labels' own text nodes, in hero coordinates. Entrance translations are removed only for that measurement and restored immediately. makeStars accepts those rectangles and rejects a home in their closed expansion by 2 + size/2 + 0.5 * STARS.parallax * (0.12 + 0.88 * d*d) CSS px. Only rejected x/y values change, using the first clear channel pair 40+2k and 41+2k. All 33 requested replacements use k=0. The other 1,750 requested homes are exact matches. Count, order, depth, size, mix, red flag and mass are exact matches for all 1,783 stars.

The field controller passes the rectangles when creating homes and remeasures when font/layout geometry changes. The loader scene uses the same makeStars input. Its main-thread bridge sends rectangles in initial/resize messages to the worker or fallback painter. The fonts and final-frame boot gates remeasure and send the font-ready rectangles before posting their boot milestone; FIFO delivery updates the worker scene before readiness can start the burst, whose first home landing is at least 0.6 s later. The main loader landing-clock scene measures the same rectangles. The Play/Pause text is set before font-ready boot measurement so review and reduced pages measure their actual Play label. No moving-star clipping, exclusion force or label mask was added.

The 7,016-byte assignment/join/re-spacing/turn region in stars.mjs is identical to the incoming page. tools/l13c-checks.mjs retains its incoming SHA-256. Star behaviour, capture and release algorithms, mark sampling, fixed loader phases/curves, entrance schedule, artwork and 101.49089474781469 s loop are retained. Moving stars may cross labels.

## Round 19, L16

Built L16 items 1-3. The final node tools/checks.mjs exits 0 and prints all clean. Every registered negative control fails at its intended gate, including L16's historical progress-crossing rule.

### Implementation and reference constraints

Read CORRECTIONS-19.md L16, MOCK-V.md, README.md and the relevant Round 19 BUILD records before editing. Acceptance and incoming page/source/hash evidence are under .scratch/l16. Used motion-playbook for the narrow clock correction. The supplied L16 rule controls the change; no generic timing or easing defaults replace it.

loaderAt now schedules each launch from launchShare(i) / rate and uses the last real boot timestamp for readiness. The obsolete progress-crossing inverse is removed. The formed hold starts at max(last landing, last boot step), and remains Infinity while a step is missing. Honest fill and target remain integrated at the existing rate. Only the formed mark waits for loading.

The module, inline bootstrap and Blob worker all obtain the same loaderAt from loader-stars.mjs. No separate copy or tools/page.mjs edit was needed. The check wraps the existing bridge.boot in an init-script setter and reads bridge.state(performance.now()/1000) just after each boot notification, using the same first-frame origin and clamp. The page's visible output is unchanged. Reduced motion still shows the formed mark and cuts on readiness; ?t= bypass and scene-resolved burst landing, entrance and pointer handover remain.

### Every earlier changed expectation

| Earlier check | Old expectation -> new expectation |
| --- | --- |
| entrance-checks.mjs, checkLoaderTiming launch schedule | Launch at the progress crossing of each share, with pauses and unknown future launches -> every boot has all 600 launch times fixed at 1.4 * i / 599 s. No reorder. |
| checkLoaderTiming formation during incomplete/late boot | Formation follows the last progress-driven flight, or Infinity with a missing launch share -> formation is always 2.40 s and all 600 have arrived then. Missing steps still prevent hold and completion at 20 s. |
| checkLoaderTiming plateau assertion | At fonts:0, arrived counts at 1.5 and 3 s both 177; assertion says progress must not invent launches -> 214 at 1.5 s, 600 by 2.40 s and at 3 s; launches proceed on time. Fill remains .25 and holdAt remains Infinity. |
| checkLoaderTiming late frame | fonts:0, atlas:0, frame:10 holds at 11.164705882 s -> 10.000000000 s. Formation 11.164705882 -> 2.40 s; burst 11.924705882 -> 10.76 s; conservative landing 13.724705882 -> 12.56 s. |
| checkLoaderTiming fullAt diagnostic | Full displayed-progress crossing -> final real boot step, or Infinity if missing. On all-ready boot, 1.647058824 -> 0 s. Fill/target integration and reduced hard steps are unchanged. This diagnostic is not a visible all-ready phase time. |
| checkLoaderTiming history count | Six histories / 36,000 samples -> eight histories / 48,000 samples, still every 1 ms through 6 s. The existing empty history stays; fonts:0 and frame:10 histories are added. Per-history exact launch/formation/readiness gates and missing-step checks at 20 s are added. |
| checkPlayer delayed fonts | After readiness, advance 4.91 s before landing then .05 s to landing -> advance 2.96 s then the unchanged .05 s. Total wait 4.96 -> 3.01 s. The actual fixture starts its clock on its first 50 ms tick and finishes fonts at wall time 2 s, so 1.95 s of launches now count while waiting. |
| tools/checks.mjs | No L16 invocation -> run the eight real-time loads and the same eight loads with the historical launch rule; the historical rule must fail the launch-gap gate in the delayed-atlas case. |

No other earlier check expectation, tolerance, control mutation or source file changed. In particular, the blocked-first-draw fixture retains its 4.91 + .05 s wait because its first visible clock frame still follows the blocking draw. All-ready phases remain hold 2.40, shrink 2.55, compressed hold 2.90, swell 2.98, burst 3.16 and conservative landing/entrance/pointer 4.96 s. Real landing still uses the existing scene duration. The 1 s cubic flights, .15/.35/.08/.18 s wind-up phases and all burst formulas remain exact.

### Real-time browser baseline

Installed local Playwright resolution and Edge executable/default GPU flags match the other page checks. Persistent isolated profiles are under .scratch/l16. The offline pack is the built page; file/data/blob assets are allowed and all page network requests are blocked. No ?t or controlled clock is used. Desktop DPR is 1 and phone DPR is 3 with mobile/touch emulation, following MOCK-V's measuring method. A CDP session sets CPU rate 4 before navigation for the throttled load. The late probe adds exactly a 2.5 s wait immediately before bootDone('atlas').

All eight loads have 600 launches from 0 to 1.40 s, largest gap 2.337229 ms, below the unchanged 10 ms limit. All 600 arrive by 2.40 s. Hold is within the prescribed 17 ms of max(2.40, last boot step), and burst is hold + .76 s. In the delayed probe, final boot is 2.801800 s, hold 2.801800 s and burst 3.561800 s. Every frame reads all launch times and phase timestamps through the bridge's entrance phase. The evidence stores every frame's phase/arrival/progress data, every distinct launch schedule and the final full schedule.

### Negative control and retained gates

One control restores 3232c60's progress-crossing launches and full-progress readiness in memory in both packed copies, including the worker source. It reruns all eight cases against the same browser gate. The delayed-atlas case fails at L16 stream: launch gap 2381.073 ms exceeds 10 ms. Its largest gap is 2381.073 ms, with 177 then 423 stars; last launch 3.778735 s, hold 4.778735 s and burst 5.538735 s. The control's failing cases are legacy-1440-cpu4, legacy-1440-late-atlas. Ordinary legacy runs may pass on a fast machine; the delayed member makes failure deterministic. No positive gate was weakened or skipped.

The complete final suite reruns all earlier gates and controls. L13c's assignment and check file, L15's label rule, stars.mjs, star-labels.mjs, play.mjs and entrance.mjs are byte-identical to the incoming files. An independent preservation probe finds identical scenes and identical all-ready draw arrays at 14 times for each requested size (42 states); every landing/scene/drawing function following loaderAt is byte-identical. Guarded-home movement, endpoint, merge and handover checks pass in the full suite.

### Render comparison, iteration and limits

Paired incoming/revision held renders at 1.20, 2.40 and 3.26 s were compared at all three sizes with the same all-ready state. Eight of nine PNG pairs are byte-identical. The 412 x 730 explosion pair differs at one of 300,760 pixels, by one RGB level at (260,260); its paired images were inspected at reading scale and preserve the same composition, mark, stars, colours and taper treatment. The supplementary strict PNG-byte assertion stopped on that ninth pair; no perfect nine-pair pixel-identity claim is made, and no runtime change or prescribed gate adjustment was made for this one-level raster difference.

The first standalone entrance run and first full suite exposed the stale delayed-font wait. An initial 3.91 s replacement was also too long because it overlooked the fixture's first 50 ms tick; the corrected 2.96 s probe plus .05 s landing passes all 17 player cases. The first full suite reported only that old loaded assertion. Final full verification uses the corrected file. Initial logs are retained. The L16 browser harness was also corrected to judge the historical rule over the full case set, rather than assume every ordinary fast-machine load must show a gap, and to evaluate final launch timestamps rather than treat the old rule's not-yet-known future predictions as actual delayed launches.

No requested runtime item or prescribed check is left out. Reviewer agents were omitted as explicitly forbidden; no independent design approval is claimed. No git, install, network request, site build or deployment ran. PresentationEngine and E: were not accessed. Only the allowed take-2 files and ignored .scratch files were written. Every Edge context started by these checks closes in finally; final process, source and ASCII audits are recorded beside the evidence. All added Markdown text is ASCII, with the preceding BUILD record preserved.
